Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 19, Step 4. The project's tree is unchanged since the ruling in reviews/2025/transcription/dependent.md at c1e852dc; the reply's tree differs from every earlier one ruled on this line (this round's reply no longer has the multiple-support refusal). Now the reply asks dependent.lawful of every entry, where the project asks it only of a Step 4 relative only as a member of the household all year (i1040gi p. 19, Step 4), so it is unknown on qualifying children and close relatives (classm.clcw26.2.deepseek dep_b, completed: project yes); its own refusal gates on dependent.divorced_rule and dependent.married fire only on refused or partial records (for example classm.retirement.1 with other_claimant withheld); the project-refuses are its cited per-person screens (dependent TIN, i1040gi p. 22) and the project-unknowns are partial or refused records. executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.json`; 1,643 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,157 partial records; 7,119 comparisons): 6,679 of 7,119 agree; 179 candidate-unknown, 161 project-unknown, 99 project-refuses, 1 candidate-refuses; on returns the full interview completes: 75 candidate-unknown. No completed-return value difference. Project stands.

As the earlier rulings on this line: the reply asks dependent.edu of every entry with no f8863.paid gate (classm.f4952.1.deepseek dep_a, completed: unknown where the project hides the line), and refuses on its divorced_rule gate on refused records; f1.t113.dep_llc_applied is refused by the project, as the owner decided (T-113). executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.json`; 1,643 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,157 partial records; 7,119 comparisons): 3,193 of 7,119 agree; 3,649 candidate-unknown, 191 candidate-refuses, 83 project-unknown, 3 project-refuses; on returns the full interview completes: 922 candidate-unknown. Project stands.

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: dependent.one
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One (this person counted once)"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: param, ref: dependent.one}

  - id: dependent.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}

  - id: dependent.h_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Are you filing a joint return for 2025?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}

  - id: dependent.h_rel_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Son, daughter, stepchild, foster child, brother, sister, stepbrother, stepsister, half brother, half sister, or a descendant of any of them"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling_descendant"}]}

  - id: dependent.h_age_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Under age 19 and younger than you (or your spouse if filing jointly), or under age 24, a full-time student and younger than you (or your spouse), or any age and permanently and totally disabled"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: qc.age_limit}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.younger, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: qc.student_age_limit}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.student, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.younger, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.disabled, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_lived_half
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Lived with you for more than half of 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, ge: [dependent.months, {const: 7, type: Count}]}
    note: "The months input already applies the exceptions to time lived with you (temporary absences, birth, death, adoption, placement). The kidnapped-child residency rule (Pub. 501) cannot be determined from the supplied inputs and is not applied; dependent.kidnapped is not used."

  - id: dependent.h_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1: a child who meets the conditions to be your qualifying child"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_age_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.self_support, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_lived_half, {const: true}]}
    note: "Where the child is also the qualifying child of another person, the tiebreaker rules are not computed from the inputs; the filer is taken to be the one claiming the child ('If you will be claiming the child as a qualifying child, go to Step 2')."

  - id: dependent.h_qc_nojoint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child tests other than the joint return test"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_age_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.self_support, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_lived_half, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_rel_qr
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Related to you (or, on a joint return, to your spouse) in a way that doesn't require living with you all year"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent_relative"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "in_law"}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_joint, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.spouse_relative, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_qr_member
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Member of household or relationship test"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_qr, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.qr_all_year, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.lawful, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_not_qc_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Wasn't a qualifying child of any other taxpayer for 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_claimant, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_nontaxpayer, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_qr_nogross
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying relative tests other than the gross income test"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_member, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_not_qc_other, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.supported, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [dependent.multiple_support]

  - id: dependent.h_qr
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4: a person who meets the conditions to be your qualifying relative"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_nogross, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.gross_income, {param: qr.gross_income_limit}]}
    note: "The sheltered-workshop exception to the gross income test is not separately asked; the gross income input is used as given."

  - id: dependent.h_citizen_test
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "U.S. citizen, U.S. national, U.S. resident alien, or a resident of Canada or Mexico"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.canada_mexico, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_can_claim_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Filing jointly, or you can't be claimed as a dependent on someone else's 2025 return"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_joint, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [sa.dependent, {const: false}]}
    note: "sa.dependent is the return-level question whether you (or your spouse) can be claimed as a dependent; it is consulted only when not filing jointly, as in Step 2 question 4 and Step 4 question 5."

  - id: dependent.claimed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You can claim this person as a dependent"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_citizen_test, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_can_claim_any, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married]

  - id: dependent.h_qc_claimed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Your qualifying child whom you claim as a dependent"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.ctc_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3: your qualifying child qualifies you for the child tax credit"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: ctc.age_limit}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.work_ssn, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.ctc_ssn, {const: true}]}
    note: "household.ctc_ssn is read as the filer-side requirement of Step 3 question 4 (you, or your spouse if filing jointly, have an SSN valid for employment; the other spouse an SSN or ITIN)."

  - id: dependent.odc_person
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This dependent qualifies you for the credit for other dependents"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.all_tin, {const: true}]}
    note: "household.all_tin is read as Step 5 question 1 (you, and your spouse if filing jointly, have an SSN or ITIN issued or applied for by the due date)."

  - id: dependent.h_ctc_per
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$2,200 per qualifying child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ctc.per_child}

  - id: dependent.h_odc_per
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$500 per other dependent"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: odc.per_dependent}

  - id: dependent.h_actc_per
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$1,700 per qualifying child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: actc.per_child}

  - id: dependent.ctc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person's share of Schedule 8812 line 5 (line 4 × $2,200)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_ctc_per}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  - id: dependent.odc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person's share of Schedule 8812 line 7 (line 6 × $500)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_odc_per}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  - id: dependent.actc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person's share of Schedule 8812 line 16b (number of qualifying children × $1,700)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_actc_per}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  - id: dependent.hoh_test1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Test 1: your parent whom you can claim as a dependent, whose main home for all of 2025 you paid over half the cost of keeping up"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.parent_home_paid, {const: true}]}
    note: "A parent claimed under a multiple support agreement is excluded; that situation is refused (dependent.multiple_support)."

  - id: dependent.h_household_only
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Related to you only as a member of your household all year"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling_descendant"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "household_member"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "other"}]}

  - id: dependent.h_dep_for_hoh
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A person you can claim as a dependent, other than one who is your dependent only because they lived with you all year"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_household_only, {const: false}]}

  - id: dependent.hoh_test2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Test 2: lived with you for more than half of the year and is your dependent (not only because they lived with you all year) or your unmarried qualifying child"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_lived_half, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_dep_for_hoh, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.married, {const: false}]}
    note: "Items 1(a) and 4 (children of divorced or separated parents), 1(c) (multiple support) and 3 (married qualifying child) are refused through the screening inputs on dependent.claimed and dependent.h_qr_nogross."

  - id: dependent.hoh_absence
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person's time lived with you for Test 2 includes an absence that doesn't count (not reasonable to assume return, or home not kept up)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.hoh_test2, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.absence_no_return, {const: true}]}
    note: "Pub. 501: a temporary absence counts only if it is reasonable to assume the absent person will return and you continue to keep up the home. Read as a per-person flag for the household refusal gate."

  - id: dependent.eic_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child for the EIC"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_age_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.us_half, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.married, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
    note: "No support test applies for the EIC. Tiebreaker and kidnapped-child rules are not computed."

  - id: dependent.eic_ssn_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC qualifying child who has a valid SSN"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_under13
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Under age 13 for at least part of 2025 when care was paid for"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_under13, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_dc_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A qualifying child under age 13 whom you can claim as a dependent"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_under13, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_dep_relaxed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Your dependent, or would be except for gross income of $5,200 or more, a joint return, or your being claimable as a dependent"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_nojoint, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_nogross, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_citizen_test, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_dc_disabled
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A person not able to care for themselves who lived with you for more than half the year and is (or but for the listed exceptions would be) your dependent"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_lived_half, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_dep_relaxed, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.dc_qp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying person for Form 2441"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_dc_child, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_dc_disabled, {const: true}]}
    note: "The custodial-parent rule for children of divorced or separated parents is refused through dependent.divorced_rule (on dependent.claimed)."

  - id: dependent.dc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person counted as one qualifying person"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: copy, from: dependent.one}
    note: "Open: a choose cannot select a Count. The per-person count is one; the form that totals qualifying persons is expected to filter the dependents by dependent.dc_qp."

  - id: dependent.h_over12
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Care included for this person was provided when the person was over age 12"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_over12_care, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_under13, {const: false}]}
    note: "A person 14 or older at the end of 2025 was 13 or older all year; a person 13 at year end who was not under 13 when care was paid for received all care while over 12; otherwise the care-period question decides."

  - id: dependent.dc_line2d
    printed: "2(d)"
    label: "Qualified expenses you incurred and paid in 2025 for the person listed in column (a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.dc_paid}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}
    shown_when: dependent.dc_qp

  - id: dependent.dc_line2c
    printed: "2(c)"
    label: "Check here if the qualifying person was over age 12 and was disabled"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_over12, {const: true}]}
    shown_when: dependent.dc_qp

  - id: dependent.ptc_magi_counted
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This dependent's modified AGI for Form 8962 Worksheet 1-2 (only dependents required to file because of their income)"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [dependent.ptc_files, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.ptc_magi
        - guard:
            op: test
            not:
              op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.ptc_files, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero

  - id: dependent.h_edu_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "An education credit may be claimed for this dependent student (claimed, expenses paid, Form 1098-T requirement met, TIN issued by the due date)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_1098t, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_tin_issued, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_edu_over
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Assistance and refunds exceed an academic period's expenses (per-period worksheets not computed)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_over, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_aoc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Lines 23-26 direct you to complete lines 27 through 30 for this student, and the institution's EIN can be provided"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line23, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line24, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line25, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line26, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_ein, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_llc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Go to line 31 for this student"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_aoc, {const: false}]}
    note: "A student who passes lines 23-26 but for whom the institution's EIN cannot be provided cannot get the American opportunity credit; the lifetime learning credit is used instead."

  - id: dependent.h_edu_adj
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "AQEE Worksheet line 3: total adjustments (add lines 2a, 2b, and 2c)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [dependent.edu_a2025, dependent.edu_a2026, dependent.edu_refunds]}

  - id: dependent.h_aoc_qee
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "AQEE Worksheet line 1 (American opportunity credit): tuition, required fees and course materials"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [dependent.edu_inst, dependent.edu_mat]}

  - id: dependent.h_aoc_aqee
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "AQEE Worksheet line 4 (American opportunity credit). Subtract line 3 from line 1. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: dependent.h_aoc_qee, take: dependent.h_edu_adj}

  - id: dependent.h_llc_aqee
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "AQEE Worksheet line 4 (lifetime learning credit). Subtract line 3 from line 1. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: dependent.edu_inst, take: dependent.h_edu_adj}

  - id: dependent.h_aoc_cap
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: aoc.expense_cap}

  - id: dependent.h_aoc_band
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$2,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: aoc.full_band}

  - id: dependent.h_line27_fig
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses, not more than $4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [dependent.h_aoc_aqee, dependent.h_aoc_cap]}

  - id: dependent.edu_line27
    printed: "27"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions). Don't enter more than $4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_aoc, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_line27_fig}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_aoc, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}
    refuses: [dependent.h_edu_over]
    shown_when: dependent.h_aoc

  - id: dependent.edu_line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Subtract $2,000 from line 27. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: dependent.edu_line27, take: {param: aoc.full_band}}
    shown_when: dependent.h_aoc

  - id: dependent.edu_line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "Multiply line 28 by 25% (0.25)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: dependent.edu_line28, rate: {param: aoc.second_rate}}
    shown_when: dependent.h_aoc

  - id: dependent.h_line29_plus
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Add $2,000 to the amount on line 29"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [dependent.edu_line29, dependent.h_aoc_band]}

  - id: dependent.edu_line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "If line 28 is zero, enter the amount from line 27. Otherwise, add $2,000 to the amount on line 29 and enter the result"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line28, {const: 0}]}, then: dependent.edu_line27}
        - {guard: {op: test, gt: [dependent.edu_line28, {const: 0}]}, then: dependent.h_line29_plus}
    shown_when: dependent.h_aoc

  - id: dependent.edu_line31
    printed: "31"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_llc, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_llc_aqee}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_llc, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}
    refuses: [dependent.h_edu_over]
    shown_when: dependent.h_llc
```
```
